### [1] [Fast Radio Bursts and Long Period Radio Transients: Radiation Mechanisms and Propagation Effects]()
*Qu, Yuanhong*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 该论文研究FRB的触发与相干辐射机制及传播效应，提出地壳震动触发磁星磁层中的快磁声波，并通过相干逆康普顿散射产生FRB；同时在强磁化、准平行极限下计算散射截面，发现光学深度远低于1。论文还提出单极感应体磁相互作用模型中的相对论电子回旋微波激射可解释至少一些长周期射电暂现源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FRB是射电天中亮温度最高的明亮射电暴，其触发机制、辐射机制和传播效应尚待研究；需要在脉冲星式（磁星磁层内起源）与GRB式（远离中心引擎的相对论性磁化激波）两类相干辐射机制之间，利用重复FRB的偏振特征和频谱带宽等一般观测性质进行约束。此外，长周期射电暂现源是一类快速增长的相干射电源，其辐射机制也有待解释。

Method: 摘要说明工作考虑脉冲星式和GRB式两类相干辐射机制，利用重复FRB的一般观测性质（偏振特征和频谱带宽）约束物理条件；开展模拟研究非轴对称地壳震动向磁层发射Alfvén波与快磁声波；通过三维粒子模拟研究同步辐射微波激射模型的偏振和频谱；计算大振幅FRB在磁层内部产生时的散射截面与光学深度；并探索周期从分钟到小时的长周期射电暂现源。具体观测数据、望远镜、频段与时间跨度摘要未说明。

Result: 模拟表明非轴对称地壳震动会向磁层发射Alfvén波和快磁声波的混合物，FRB可由束团对低频快磁声波的相干逆康普顿散射在距磁星半径数百倍处产生。三维粒子模拟用于给出同步辐射微波激射模型的偏振和频谱；在强磁化和准平行极限下，散射截面渐近恢复线性区标度，并被相对论性粒子运动强烈抑制，光学深度远低于1。对于长周期射电暂现源，摘要指出其周期从分钟到小时、亮温度相对较低，并提出单极感应体磁相互作用模型可解释其射电辐射性质，相对论电子回旋微波激射是最可能的机制，可解释至少一些LPRTs，而短周期族群可能由慢磁星等其他引擎驱动。

Conclusion: 作者提出地壳震动可触发磁星磁层中的突然瞬变，并通过束团对低频快磁声波的相干逆康普顿散射产生FRB；强磁化、准平行极限下的散射截面与光学深度计算支持磁层内大振幅FRB的传播分析。作者还提出，磁化WD与低磁化RD之间的单极感应体磁相互作用模型结合相对论电子回旋微波激射，可解释至少一些长周期射电暂现源，而短周期LPRT族群可能由慢磁星等其他引擎驱动。上述结论直接来自摘要所述框架；关于其对具体FRB、磁星或FRB–磁星关联的可迁移性，根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: Fast Radio Bursts (FRBs) are bright radio bursts with the highest brightness temperatures in the radio sky. In this thesis, we investigate the trigger mechanisms, radiation mechanisms, and propagation effects of FRBs. Two general classes of coherent radiation mechanisms are considered in two categories: pulsar-like models invoking emission originating within the magnetar magnetosphere, and GRB-like models, which attribute the emission to relativistic magnetized shocks occurring far away from the central engine. We use the general observed properties (polarization features and spectra bandwidths) of repeating FRBs to constrain the physical conditions needed for these two mechanisms. We propose that crust quakes are invoked as a mechanism to trigger sudden transients in the magnetospheres of magnetars. Our simulations show that non-axisymmetric quakes will launch a mixture of Alfvén and fast magnetosonic waves into the magnetosphere. In this framework, FRBs are generated via coherent inverse Compton scattering (ICS) off low-frequency fast magnetosonic waves by bunches at a distance of a few hundred times the magnetar radius. We also investigate the polarization and spectra of the synchrotron maser model from three-dimensional particle-in-cell simulations. As large-amplitude FRBs are produced inside the magnetosphere, we compute the scattering cross section and the corresponding optical depth. In the strongly magnetized and quasi-parallel limits, the cross sections asymptotically recover the linear regime scalings and are strongly suppressed by relativistic particle motion, leading to optical depths well below unity. In addition, we explore long-period radio transients (LPRTs), a rapidly growing class of coherent radio sources with periods ranging from minutes to hours and relatively low brightness temperatures. We suggest that the radio emission properties of the system can be well explained within the framework of the unipolar inductor magnetic interaction model between the magnetized WD and the RD with low magnetization, with a relativistic version of electron cyclotron maser emission being the most likely radiation mechanism. We suggest that this mechanism can interpret at least some long-period radio transients. The short-period population of LPRTs is likely powered by other engines such as slow magnetars.

</details>


### [2] [Spectro-Polarimetric Properties of CHIME FRB Sources]()
*Zhou, Dengke,Feng, Yi,Xu, Jiaying,Xu, Chenyuan,Fang, Jianhua*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 利用CHIME观测，作者系统分析了重复与非重复FRB的谱偏振性质：为28个重复FRB测量了 $\sigma_{\rm RM}$，将已知样本从14个扩展到36个源；重复群体KDE峰值在 $1.3\ \mathrm{rad\ m^{-2}}$，约70%的源 $\sigma_{\rm RM}\gtrsim 1\ \mathrm{rad\ m^{-2}}$，而70个非重复FRB中无一表现出 $\sigma_{\rm RM}\gtrsim 5\ \mathrm{rad\ m^{-2}}$ 的显著退偏振。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低频率退偏振由参数 $\sigma_{\rm RM}$ 量化，可探测FRB前身星周围复杂的磁电离介质，并已在重复FRB群体中被观测到。摘要指出该参数为理解FRB起源与环境提供关键线索，但重复源的已测量样本有限；作者希望扩展测量样本并对重复与非重复FRB进行系统的谱偏振分析。

Method: 利用加拿大氢强度测绘实验（CHIME）的观测，对重复与非重复FRB开展系统的谱偏振分析。对28个重复FRB测量 $\sigma_{\rm RM}$；对70个非重复FRB研究四个谱偏振模型；并采用核密度估计（KDE）刻画重复群体的 $\sigma_{\rm RM}$ 分布。具体观测频段、时间跨度与数据处理细节摘要未说明。

Result: 28个重复FRB的 $\sigma_{\rm RM}$ 测量将已知样本从14个源扩展到36个源，增加2.6倍。重复群体KDE峰值位于 $1.3\ \mathrm{rad\ m^{-2}}$，约70%的源 $\sigma_{\rm RM}\gtrsim 1\ \mathrm{rad\ m^{-2}}$。70个非重复FRB中，没有源表现出 $\sigma_{\rm RM}\gtrsim 5\ \mathrm{rad\ m^{-2}}$ 的显著退偏振，约一半与跨频率恒定的线偏振度一致。作者提醒这些结果可能受CHIME有限频率覆盖影响。

Conclusion: 作者认为，重复群体中约70%的源 $\sigma_{\rm RM}\gtrsim 1\ \mathrm{rad\ m^{-2}}$ 意味着大多数重复FRB处于复杂的磁电离环境中。对于非重复FRB，未发现显著退偏振，且约一半与恒定线偏振度一致，但这一结果可能受CHIME有限频率覆盖的观测偏差影响。作者提出，未来跨广泛分离频率的超宽带偏振测量将克服当前观测偏差，实现精确的 $\sigma_{\rm RM}$ 测量，并显著加深对FRB环境的理解。根据摘要无法判断该工作与脉冲星、磁星或快速射电暴-磁星关联的具体物理联系。

Abstract: Fast radio bursts (FRBs) are enigmatic millisecond-duration radio transients whose polarization properties offer crucial insights into their origins and environments. In particular, low-frequency depolarization---quantified by the parameter <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:semantics><mml:msub><mml:mi>σ</mml:mi><mml:mi>RM</mml:mi></mml:msub></mml:semantics></mml:math></inline-formula>---probes the complex magneto-ionic medium surrounding the progenitor, and has been observed across a population of repeating FRBs. We present a systematic spectro-polarimetric analysis of repeating and non-repeating FRBs using observations from the Canadian Hydrogen Intensity Mapping Experiment (CHIME). For 28 repeating FRBs, we measure <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:semantics><mml:msub><mml:mi>σ</mml:mi><mml:mi>RM</mml:mi></mml:msub></mml:semantics></mml:math></inline-formula>, expanding the known sample from 14 to 36 sources (an increase by a factor of 2.6). The kernel density estimate (KDE) of the repeating population peaks at <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:semantics><mml:mrow><mml:mn>1.3</mml:mn><mml:mspace></mml:mspace><mml:mi>rad</mml:mi><mml:mspace></mml:mspace><mml:msup><mml:mi>m</mml:mi><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup></mml:mrow></mml:semantics></mml:math></inline-formula>, with approximately 70% of the sources showing <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:semantics><mml:mrow><mml:msub><mml:mi>σ</mml:mi><mml:mi>RM</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>≳</mml:mo><mml:mn>1</mml:mn><mml:mspace></mml:mspace><mml:mi>rad</mml:mi><mml:mspace></mml:mspace><mml:msup><mml:mi>m</mml:mi><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup></mml:mrow></mml:semantics></mml:math></inline-formula>, implying that most reside in complex magneto-ionic environments. For 70 non-repeating FRBs, we investigate four spectro-polarimetric models; no source exhibits significant depolarization with <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:semantics><mml:mrow><mml:msub><mml:mi>σ</mml:mi><mml:mi>RM</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>≳</mml:mo><mml:mn>5</mml:mn><mml:mspace></mml:mspace><mml:mi>rad</mml:mi><mml:mspace></mml:mspace><mml:msup><mml:mi>m</mml:mi><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup></mml:mrow></mml:semantics></mml:math></inline-formula>. Roughly half of the non-repeaters are consistent with a constant linear polarization fraction across frequency. We caution, however, that these results may be affected by the limited frequency coverage of CHIME. Future ultra-wideband polarimetry, spanning widely separated frequencies, will overcome current observational biases, enable precise <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:semantics><mml:msub><mml:mi>σ</mml:mi><mml:mi>RM</mml:mi></mml:msub></mml:semantics></mml:math></inline-formula> measurements, and substantially deepen our understanding of FRB environments.

</details>


### [3] [The magnetar fraction in core-collapse supernovae]()
*Pardo-Araujo, Celsa,Rea, Nanda,Ronchi, Michele,Graber, Vanessa*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 通过对银河系孤立中子星类别进行包含动力学、自转减慢与磁热演化的族群合成，作者得出磁星在 B<SUB>0</SUB> ≈ 1-2.5 × 10<SUP>14</SUP> G 处达到峰值并平均约占孤立中子星族群的 ~50%，且重现观测族群需要大于每世纪 2 次的核心坍缩超新星发生率以及比先前推断更大的磁星比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁星是超强磁场驱动的极端中子星，被视为超亮超新星、伽马射线暴和快速射电暴等强大河外暂现源的有力引擎；但其相对于普通中子星的形成率仍不确定，常妨碍与这些暂现源发生率的直接比较。此外，磁星近来被表明与其他中子星类别存在演化关联，这对估计中子星族群中的确切磁星比例至关重要；而观测到的年轻（&lt;2 kyr）银河系中子星族群中，23 个探测源仅有 9 个（40%）为经典自转供能脉冲星，其余 60% 为磁星或中央致密天体。

Method: 摘要说明采用对银河系中孤立中子星类别的族群合成，研究核心坍缩超新星中的磁星诞生比例，其中包含动力学演化、自转减慢和磁热演化。摘要未说明具体的观测数据、望远镜或仪器、观测频段、时间跨度、样本量以及统计分析方法。

Result: 摘要报告：在观测到的年轻（&lt;2 kyr）银河系中子星族群中，23 个探测到的源中只有 9 个（40%）是经典的自转供能脉冲星，其余 60% 是磁星或中央致密天体。在假设双峰初始磁场分布的族群合成中，磁星在 B<SUB>0</SUB> ≈ 1-2.5 × 10<SUP>14</SUP> G 处达到峰值，并平均约占孤立中子星族群的 ~50%。摘要指出，重现所观测到的族群需要大于每世纪 2 次的核心坍缩超新星发生率，以及比先前推断更大的磁星比例。

Conclusion: 作者明确得出结论：重现所观测到的族群需要大于每世纪 2 次的核心坍缩超新星发生率，以及比先前推断更大的磁星比例。摘要未说明该结果对快速射电暴、脉冲星单脉冲发射、脉冲轮廓演化、模式转换、计时、偏振或射电频谱的具体推论；根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: Magnetars are extreme neutron stars powered by ultra-strong magnetic fields (≳10<SUP>14</SUP> G), and they are compelling engines for some of the most powerful extragalactic transients, such as superluminous supernovae, gamma-ray bursts and fast radio bursts. Yet their formation rate relative to ordinary neutron stars remains uncertain, which often prevents direct comparisons with the rates of these transients. Furthermore, magnetars have recently been shown to be evolutionarily related to other neutron star claßes, an eßential component in estimating the exact magnetar fraction within the neutron star population. In the observed young (&lt;2 kyr) Galactic neutron star population, only 9 of 23 detected sources (40%) are claßical rotation-powered pulsars. The remaining 60% are magnetars or central compact objects. Here we study the magnetar birth fraction in core-collapse supernovae using a population synthesis of isolated neutron star claßes in our Galaxy, including dynamical evolution, spin-down and magneto-thermal evolution. By aßuming a bimodal initial magnetic field distribution, magnetars peak at B<SUB>0</SUB> ≈ 1-2.5 × 10<SUP>14</SUP> G and represent on average ~50% of the isolated neutron star population. We conclude that reproducing the observed population requires a core-collapse supernova rate larger than 2 per century and a larger magnetar fraction than previously inferred.

</details>


### [4] [Modeling natal kicks in the formation of binary pulsars: Impact on the orbital dynamics and population characteristics]()
*Taani, Ali*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 对 10000 个双星的蒙特卡洛模拟表明，AIC 期间 10 至 100 km/s 的适度踢速度可再现观测到的密近双星轨道分布范围，而 ≲50 km/s 的踢使系统以高概率保持束缚，更强的踢则使很大一部分双星解体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 吸积诱导坍缩（AIC）期间产生的踢速度能显著改变双脉冲星的动力学，但 AIC 之后轨道的重塑方式以及双星在此事件中幸存所需的条件尚需明确，尤其是踢幅度如何影响短周期双脉冲星族群仍不清楚。

Method: 对 10000 个双星进行蒙特卡洛模拟；通过要求伴星在坍缩时刻的位置同时与踢前和踢后的轨迹一致，对轨道响应进行建模；考察 AIC 后周期、踢幅度与所诱导偏心率之间的关系。摘要未说明观测数据、仪器、频率或时间跨度。

Result: 10 至 100 km/s 的适度踢能再现观测到的密近双星轨道分布范围；AIC 后周期表明两者之比敏感地依赖于踢幅度和诱导的偏心率；现今周期低于 2d 的双星中约 23% 源自尺度相近的致密 AIC 前系统；踢 ≤50 km/s 的系统以高概率保持束缚，而更强的踢使很大一部分双星解体。

Conclusion: 作者认为 AIC 过程驱动了显著的轨道演化，并在塑造短周期双脉冲星族群中发挥作用；这些结果阐明了 AIC 经由哪些动力学路径对中子星形成作出贡献，以及双脉冲星轨道的多样性。根据摘要无法判断其对磁星、快速射电暴（FRB）或单脉冲观测的具体联系。

Abstract: Kick velocities produced during accretion induced collapse (AIC) can substantially alter the dynamics of binary pulsars. Here we examine how these kicks reshape post-AIC orbits and identify the conditions under which binaries survive this event. We modeled the orbital response by requiring that the companion's location at the moment of collapse be consistent with both the pre- and post-kick trajectories. Monte Carlo simulations of 10000 binaries show that modest kicks of 10 to 100 km/s reproduce the observed population spread in the orbitals of close binaries. Post-AIC periods show that the ratio of the two depends sensitively on kick amplitude and the induced eccentricity. Roughly 23% of binaries with present day periods below 2d originate from compact pre-AIC systems of similar scale. Systems experiencing kicks <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mo>&lt;=</mml:mo></mml:math></inline-formula> 50 km/s remain bound with high probability, whereas stronger kicks disrupt a large fraction of binaries. significant orbital evolution driven by the AIC process and its role in shaping the population of short-period binary pulsars. These results clarify the dynamical pathways through which AIC contributes to neutron star formation and the diversity of binary pulsar orbits.

</details>


### [5] [Probing the Baryonic Feedback Effect with CSST Weak Lensing and Future Fast Radio Burst Measurements]()
*Feng, Shuai,Gong, Yan,Xiong, Qi,Liu, Xiaohui,Lin, Hengjie,Hu, Dingao,Chen, Xuelei*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 该文通过模拟研究表明，联合CSST弱引力透镜与未来SKA、DSA-2000的FRB DM统计，可将重子反馈参数 \(\log_{10} T_{\ \text{AGN}}\) 约束到0.4%，并给出 \(\sum m_{\nu} < 0.47\ \text{eV}\) 的中微子质量总和上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注重子反馈效应的探测问题，并指出未来CSST测光巡天弱引力透镜测量与SKA、DSA-2000等下一代射电望远镜的FRB DM统计可用于联合探测；其动机是借助这两类未来观测获得对重子反馈参数、中微子质量及其他重要宇宙学参数的约束。摘要未说明具体的知识空白表述。

Method: 采用重子晕模型计算物质、电子以及物质—电子功率谱；基于CSST、SKA和DSA-2000的设计，在考虑真实噪声和系统效应的情况下生成模拟数据；随后用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）方法分析模拟数据以研究参数约束，并进行融入FRB DM测量的 \(3 \times 2\ \text{pt}(e, m)\) 分析。摘要未说明观测频率、时间跨度、样本量等细节。

Result: 仅CSST弱引力透镜可将重子反馈参数 \(\log_{10} T_{\ \text{AGN}}\) 约束到3.1%的精度，并给出 \(\sum m_{\nu} < 0.53\ \text{eV}\)；在进行 \(3 \times 2\ \text{pt}(e, m)\) 分析时，纳入FRB DM测量可显著将 \(\log_{10} T_{\ \text{AGN}}\) 的精度提高到0.4%，并通过有效打破简并，将 \(\sum m_{\nu}\) 的上限改善为 \(< 0.47\ \text{eV}\)。

Conclusion: 作者认为，未来FRB DM与弱引力透镜巡天的联合观测是探测重子反馈效应的有力工具，有助于获得对中微子质量及其他重要宇宙学参数的稳健约束。该结论直接支持的是重子反馈与宇宙学参数约束方面的方法学价值；对于该工作与脉冲星、磁星、单脉冲发射、脉冲轮廓演化、模式转换、计时、自转减慢、偏振或FRB—磁星联系等主题的直接关联，摘要未说明，根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: We explore the joint probe on the baryonic feedback effect using the weak-lensing measurement from the upcoming Chinese Space Station Survey Telescope (CSST) photometric survey and the dispersion measure (DM) statistics of the fast radio bursts (FRBs) from next-generation radio telescopes, i.e., the Square Kilometre Array (SKA) and the Deep Synoptic Array 2000 (DSA-2000). By employing the baryonic halo model, we compute the matter, electron, and matter--electron power spectra and generate mock data considering realistic noise and systematic effects based on the designs of the telescopes. These mock data are then analyzed using the Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) method to investigate the parameter constraints. In Chapter 5, we use Monte Carlo simulations to study whether there are genuine non-repeating FRBs. In the fallback disc model, it was suggested that most INS populations pass through an RRAT phase during their evolution, and the mechanism producing these bursts could be the enhancement of the flux of open magnetic field lines through the poles of dead pulsars due to penetration of the inner disc into the light cylinder. To test this idea, we determine the RRAT phases that could be produced by this mechanism along the evolutionary curves of the INS families on the period-period derivative (P - <inline-formula><tex-math id="TM0001" notation="LaTeX">$\dot{P}$</tex-math></inline-formula>) diagram. These RRAT phases form an RRAT region in the P-<inline-formula><tex-math id="TM0002" notation="LaTeX">$\dot{P}$</tex-math></inline-formula> diagram, which is in agreement with the known RRAT distribution. Using our results, we also investigate the descendants of central compact objects (CCOs). We estimate that the CCOs similar to the three CCOs with measured P and <inline-formula><tex-math id="TM0003" notation="LaTeX">$\dot{P}$</tex-math></inline-formula> are not likely to become radio pulsars (RPs). The situation is rather different for Calvera, a recently identified CCO. In the model, Calvera-like sources become RPs with lifetimes &gt;10<SUP>8</SUP> yr. Indeed, there is a cluster of RPs in the region of P - <inline-formula><tex-math id="TM0004" notation="LaTeX">$\dot{P}$</tex-math></inline-formula> diagram where descendants of Calvera-like CCOs evolve in our model. We also estimate that they have a very low birth rate, and therefore their immediate descendants may not currently exist in our Galaxy.

Abstract: One of the most extreme objects in the Universe, neutron stars are the remnants of supernova explosions. A usual neutron star has a mass in the range of 0.8 − 2.4M⊙ and a radius of about 10 km. Such small radii and high masses imply extreme densities, that exceed the nuclear density ρnuc . The extreme conditions of cold ultradense matter present inside the neutron star cannot be achieved in terrestrial laboratories, so the equation of state (EOS) of such matter is not well understood. Currently, there are many theoretical models of the EOS based on nuclear physics (Quantum Chromodynamics (QCD), chiral Effective Field Theory (EFT), etc) or on astrophysical observations (pulsar mass measurements, gravitational wave detections, X-ray observations, etc). Based on these models, researchers have described the behavior of binary neutron star mergers, the resulting gravitational wave signals, and the intrinsic parameters (mass, radius, spin, tidal deformability) corresponding to each model. Using the gravitational wave data from LIGO and Virgo, we can infer the EOS of neutron stars by comparing the observed signals with the theoretical models. For this purpose, we use the Rapid parameter inference on gravitational wave sources via Iterative FiTting (RIFT) code. This code utilizes Bayesian statistics to infer the intrinsic parameters of the neutron stars from the observed gravitational wave events. In this work, we expand the RIFT code to perform EOS inference using EOS tables from Legred et al 2021. To implement this feature, we calculate local ordering statistic S (corresponding to the tidal deformability Λ̃ for equal-mass binary) along with other intrinsic parameters at each step of the iterative process. Then, we use this parameter to pinpoint the most probable EOS model for each sample. This results in a posterior distribution of EOS models for the analyzed event which are linked to the tables of Pressure P vs Baryon density ρ and Mass M vs Radius R. Using the posterior samples generated by RIFT, we calculate 90% credible intervals for the P (ρ) and M (R) relations. The benefits of this update to the RIFT code are twofold: it allows fast and reliable EOS inference from GW events and it refines the regular RIFT analysis by ensuring the consistency of the parameter space with realistic EOS models. As a test case for this study, we repeat the analysis of the GW170817 event and compare the results with the previous studies (original LIGO parameter inference 2020 and Legred et. al 2021 EOS inference). We experiment with different approximations (assuming no spin, using high and similar mas ratio test cases) to assess the performance of our method under different conditions. Our results align well with the reference studies and show potential for further studies on other GW events.

</details>

Abstract: The extreme environment within neutron stars presents the opportunity to probe the nuclear equation of state at high densities and study the nature of stellar remnants near 1 solar mass. Parameter inference for the equation of state has received abundant attention in previous studies, but only a fraction of those have employed nontrivial population models in their priors. In this work, we introduce an upgrade to the generalized version of the popular RIFT algorithm known as HyperPipe, enabling the inclusion of flexible, user-provided priors within preexisting likelihood marginalization scripts that will facilitate joint inference of the equation of state and binary neutron star population hyperparameters. We demonstrate the utility of this pipeline framework with a comprehensive test involving data from double neutron star systems, massive galactic pulsars, isolated X-ray pulsars, and gravitational wave events, utilizing a truncated bivariate normal distribution as our population prior model. We assess the outcome of this inference and provide comparisons with previous results. iii

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲星故障行为多样，常被定性标记为“Vela-like”或“Crab-like”，但这些类别缺乏可重复的定量边界。因此需要一个客观、可量化的分类方案，以刻画故障活动的不同模式。

Result: 层次聚类揭示两个形态类别：紧凑类对应较小 HDR 面积、升高的各向异性和有限的边缘占据；延展类对应更宽的 HDR 占据和更强的低密度延展。bootstrap 重采样和超参数敏感性检验对大多数源确认了这一划分，但 PSR J0537-6910 和 PSR J1341-6220 处于中间位置。logistic 回归显示平均回溯等待时间 &lt;∆t<SUB>-</SUB>&gt; 是最强类别预测因子（样本内 AUC = 0.88；留一交叉验证 AUC<SUB>CV</SUB> = 0.59，反映有限样本量）。使用 ∆t<SUB>+</SUB> 的平行分析恢复了相同两类别结构，但稳定性降低。

Conclusion: 数据表明紧凑类与接近完全的储库耗竭一致，而延展类反映部分的、雪崩式的释放；因此，对延展类脉冲星中 G 的短期测量可能低估真实的壳层超流分数。根据摘要无法判断该分类与磁星、快速射电暴（FRB）或单脉冲辐射等主题的直接物理联系。

Abstract: Pulsar glitches display a wide variety of behaviors. They are commonly labeled as ``Vela-like'' or ``Crab-like,'' yet these qualitative categories do not provide reproducible quantitative boundaries. In this work, we constructed an objective classification scheme based on the joint distribution of backward waiting time ∆t<SUB>-</SUB> and fractional glitch amplitude ∆ν/ν. A consolidated sample of 349 glitches from 22 pulsars was compiled by crossmatching the Jodrell Bank and ATNF glitch databases. For each source, we applied two-dimensional kernel density estimation (KDE) together with highest-density regions (HDRs) to derive geometric descriptors, including the HDR area, anisotropy ratio, and out-of-HDR fraction. Hierarchical clustering of the KDE--HDR descriptors revealed two morphology classes independent of visual inspection. In this convention, the compact class is associated with small HDR areas, elevated anisotropy, and limited peripheral occupancy. The extended class is associated with broader HDR occupancy and stronger low-density extensions. We found that bootstrap resampling and hyperparameter sensitivity tests confirmed this partition for most sources. Nevertheless, two prolific pulsars (PSR J0537-6910 and PSR J1341-6220) were found to occupy intermediate positions. Logistic regression identified the mean backward waiting time &lt;∆t<SUB>-</SUB>&gt; as the strongest class predictor (in-sample area under the curve AUC = 0.88; leave-one-out cross-validated AUC<SUB>CV</SUB> = 0.59, reflecting the limited sample size). Additionally, a parallel analysis using ∆t<SUB>+</SUB> recovered the same two-class structure but with reduced stability. The data suggest that the compact class is consistent with near-complete reservoir depletion, whereas the extended class reflects partial, avalanche-like releases. As a result, short-term measurements of G in extended class pulsars may underestimate the true crustal superfluid fraction.

</details>

Abstract: My dissertation includes two parts: <P />Physics of Gamma-Ray Bursts (GRBs): Gamma-ray bursts are multi-wavelength transients, with both prompt gamma-ray emission and late time afterglow emission observed by telescopes in different wavelengths. I have carried out three investigations to understand GRB prompt emission and afterglow. Chapter 2 develops a new method, namely, "Stepwise Filter Correlation" method, to decompose the variability components in a light curve. After proving its reliability through simulations, we apply this method to 266 bright GRBs and find that the majority of the bursts have clear evidence of superposition of fast and slow variability components. Chapter 3 gives a complete presentation of the analytical approximations for synchrotron self-compton emission for all possible orders of the characteristic synchrotron spectral breaks (nu_a, nu_m, and nu_c). We identify a "strong absorption" regime when nu_a &gt; nu_c, and derive the critical condition for this regime. The external shock theory is an elegant theory to model GRB afterglows. It invokes a limit number of model parameters, and has well predicted spectral and temporal properties. Chapter 4 gives a complete reference of all the analytical synchrotron external shock afterglow models by deriving the temporal and spectral indices of all the models in all spectral regimes. This complete reference will serve as a useful tool for afterglow observers to quickly identify relevant models to interpret their data and identify new physics when the models fail. <P />Milti-messenger signals from double neutron star merger: As the multi-messenger era of astronomy ushers in, the second part of the dissertation studies the possible electromagnetic (EM) and neutrino emission counterparts of double neutron star mergers. Chapter 6 suggests that if double neutron star mergers leave behind a massive magnetar rather than a black hole, the magnetar wind could push the ejecta launched during the merger process, and under certain conditions, accelerates it to a relativistic speed. Such a magnetar-powered ejecta, when interacting with the ambient medium, would develop a bright broad-band afterglow due to external shock synchrotron radiation. We study this physical scenario in detail, and present the predicted X-ray, optical and radio light curves for a range of magnetar and ejecta parameters. Chapter 7 applies the model to interpret one optical transient discovered recently. In chapter 8, we show that protons accelerated in the external shock would interact with photons generated in the dissipating magnetar wind and emit high energy neutrinos and photons. We find that PeV neutrinos could be emitted from the shock front as long as the ejecta could be accelerated to a relativistic speed. These events would contribute to the diffuse Pev neutrino background and sub-Tev gamma-ray background.

</details>

The new catalog contains 1254 SGR short bursts observed over 17 yr, providing the longest uninterrupted, high-sensitivity, all-sky monitoring of magnetar bursts with unprecedented spectral and temporal resolution. Our catalog contains bursts from 17 unique Galactic sources, with major contributions by bursts from SGR J1935+2154 and SGR J1550--5418. Abstract: Magnetar short bursts (SBs) are hard X-ray transients of durations 0.01--1 s peaking at ~10--100 keV and are prime targets for new high-energy missions and polarimeters. The recent association of SBs with bright radio bursts in SGR 1935+2154 has broadened interest in SB physics. We present new advanced fireball models combining general relativistic light bending, polarized transport in magnetized photospheres, magnetic photon-splitting attenuation, and magnetospheric vacuum birefringence. These models also have relevance to trapped fireballs in magnetar giant flare pulsating tails. We adopt confined flux-tube geometries consistent with adiabatic fireballs, anisotropic/polarized emergent intensities to produce spectra and polarizations, and energy--time Stokes impulse responses. We predict that most fireballs are highly linearly polarized, especially when vacuum birefringence is important. There is rich potential for diagnostics: coexisting direct and lensed delayed images, gaps by occultation of the neutron star surface, and Shapiro+Rømer delay with temporal caustics. These effects can imprint spin-phase dependence of the spectral and polarization character of bursts. Predicted signatures depend strongly on viewing geometry, fireball configuration, and photon-splitting assumptions, yielding large variance in model high-energy spectral shapes and cutoffs, and energy-dependent polarization. The models can reproduce established double-blackbody SB spectral phenomenology, and we find that the unusual 2020 April radio-associated SB from SGR 1935+2154 is broadly consistent with a footpoint close to the magnetic pole, and possibly near pole-on viewing geometry. Our models motivate reverberation-style analyses for SBs and suggest that high-quality data might constrain source geometry, burst crustal footpoints, and, potentially, neutron star masses and radii.

Abstract: The detection of gravitational waves (GWs) with ground-based interferometers like the Laser Interferometer Gravitational Wave Observatory (LIGO) revolutionized our view of the Universe. Pulsar timing arrays (PTAs) like observed by the North American Nanohertz Observatory for Gravitational Waves (NANOGrav) will provide the community with an avenue for exploring the GW spectrum beyond what is capable with ground-based interferometers. Unlike LIGO, pulsars do not exist in an ideal environment where clever engineering can mitigate sources of noise. With that being said, many of the dominant sources of noise in PTAs have been well modeled within NANOGrav. The impact of red spin noise (RSN), which may result from rotational instabilities in the neutron star itself, is particularly problematic because its effect on a PTA's timing residuals could resemble the effects of a gravitational wave background (GWB). <P />In this work, we simulate a PTA where each pulsar suffers from varying amplitudes of RSN and also has an underlying GWB. We recover the parameters describing the RSN to better characterize how a GWB of varying amplitudes biases our recovery efforts. We find regions of parameter space where the GWB and the RSN heavily bias each other. In these regions, RSN could masquerade as a GWB, or vice versa. However, we also find regions of parameter space that do not result in a biased recovery, even in the case of a large GWB amplitude. Finally, we define the problematic regions of parameter space and draw conclusions about recovery efforts in today's operational PTAs.

</details>

Abstract: Continuous gravitational waves, i.e., persistent and nearly monochromatic signals emitted by asymmetric spinning neutron stars, remain elusive. Searches for these signals from unknown binary systems are the most computationally challenging, but they are essential, given that binary accretion provides a natural mechanism for creating the required asymmetry, and around half of the known pulsars rotating above 25 Hz are part of a binary system. Here we report on a search of a large uncharted parameter-space region: for the first time we cover gravitational-wave frequencies above 520 Hz (from 50 to 1000 Hz), and, for the first time with advanced detectors, orbital periods lower than 3 days are explored. No signal is detected, and we set the most stringent constraints to date on the amplitude of signals of this kind. Our results exclude with 95% confidence neutron stars within 100 pc and rotating faster than ~495 Hz from having ellipticities above 5.2 × 10<SUP>-8</SUP>. Within the same distance our results also exclude r-mode amplitudes above 1.5 × 10<SUP>-6</SUP> for stars rotating faster than ~740 Hz.

Abstract: Broadband radio emission originates from a wide range of astronomical sources, such as the hot plasma of the middle solar corona and the exotic environments found around objects such as radio pulsars. For many of these sources, radio emission mechanisms and regions are not well understood, as they are complex and obscured from direct observation by the ionized media that the emission must traverse as it radiates outward toward the observer. Broadband radio waves from galactic astrophysical sources are delayed and distorted on their paths toward the Earth as they pass through the free electrons that compose a variety of media, some of which include the interstellar medium (ISM), the solar atmosphere, and the ionosphere of our planet. These effects can be significant, from obscuring features of the emission mechanisms to rendering the objects almost undetectable. Radio emissions from astrophysical objects can be employed to both study the sources themselves and probe the media through which the emission travels on its path toward the Earth. In this thesis work, approaches from across modern radio astronomy were employed to study the ionosphere, the middle solar corona, the ISM, and emissions from radio pulsars. A low-frequency radio telescope array was commissioned in the path of totality of the 2024 total solar eclipse to probe the region of emission at 42 MHz in the middle solar corona and observe the ionospheric response to the eclipse. A CLEAN deconvolution algorithm, written in Python, was developed, parameterized, and implemented to both rebuild intrinsic pulses of radio pulsars and probe the composition of the ISM along their line of sight toward the Earth. Finally, a tree dedispersion algorithm, implemented on field programmable gate arrays (FPGAs), was developed and tested to account for the dispersive effects of the ISM on radio transients such as pulsars and fast radio bursts.

</details>

Abstract: We present a semi-analytical model of magnetized accretion disks around neutron stars that extends previous disk-magnetosphere interaction frameworks by including Hall diffusion and disk-driven mass outflows. The modified induction and angular momentum equations are solved numerically to investigate the rotational structure of the disk. The results show that Hall diffusion significantly alters the angular momentum transport within the disk, broadening the transition region between Keplerian rotation and corotation with the neutron star. In contrast, the impact of disk outflows is twofold and critically depends on the specific angular momentum extracted from or delivered to the disk by the outflows, as parameterized by <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>l</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula>. Outflows that remove angular momentum from the disk (<inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mi>l</mml:mi><mml:mo>&lt;</mml:mo><mml:mn>0</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula>) reduce the fastness parameter and broaden the transition zone, while outflows that supply angular momentum to the disk (<inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mi>l</mml:mi><mml:mo>&gt;</mml:mo><mml:mn>0</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula>) increase the fastness parameter and narrow the transition zone. When both effects of Hall diffusion and outflows operate together, they establish a competitive interplay that dictates the final disk configuration. Hall diffusion tends to expand the region of sub-Keplerian rotation, while outflows can either counteract or reinforce this broadening depending on the sign and magnitude of <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>l</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula>. These findings highlight the importance of non-ideal MHD processes and outflows in determining the structure and dynamics of accretion disks around magnetized compact objects.

Abstract: We develop a new analytical model describing the radiative and dynamical structure of an accretion-powered X-ray pulsar (XRP), including relativistic effects and a detailed representation of the rotational and magnetic geometry of the neutron star and the two accretion columns. The model provides for the first time a simultaneous calculation of both the phase-averaged spectrum and the pulse profile for an accretion-powered XRP that can be used to analyze observational data. The X-ray continuum spectrum is calculated using the analytical model of P. A. Becker &amp; M. T. Wolff, which assumes a conical accretion column geometry. The trajectory of the radiation is tracked through the curved spacetime surrounding the neutron star using the Schwarzschild metric. The angular distribution of the radiation escaping from the surface of a column (the beaming pattern) is represented using a set of ``laser-like'' emission directions, with associated amplitudes, called weight coefficients, that determine the contributions of the corresponding ``subprofiles'' to the observed pulse profile. The subprofiles provide basis functions that are used to fit the observed pulse profile. The resulting set of weight coefficients determine the beaming pattern of the emission from the accretion column. We use the new model to analyze NuSTAR data for Her X-1, which allows us to determine the temperature, accretion rate, and magnetic field strength, as well as the inclination angle of the neutron star's spin axis and the rotational latitudes of the two magnetic poles. The procedure also yields the beaming pattern of the emission, hence providing for the first time a self-consistent phenomenological description of the physical and radiative structures of the two accretion columns.

Abstract: In recent years, NICER data have been extensively used to determine neutron star masses and radii via pulse profile modeling. Pulse profile modeling is implemented with the X-PSI package, and the best-fit parameters are typically obtained using Bayesian inference. Using simulated data, we demonstrate the first ever application of frequentist inference to determine the neutron star radius, where the nuisance parameters are treated using profile likelihood. We find that the profile likelihood technique can recover the true radius to &lt;<inline-formula><mml:math><mml:semantics><mml:mrow><mml:mn>1</mml:mn><mml:mi>σ</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:semantics></mml:math></inline-formula>. The uncertainty in the estimated radius is also comparable to that obtained from Bayesian analysis while being computationally much faster. Therefore, this work serves as a proof-of-principle application of frequentist inference to estimate neutron star radii using pulse profile modeling and complements the Bayesian inference technique currently used. We have also made our analysis codes for frequentist inference using X-PSI publicly available.

Abstract: Burst oscillations observed during thermonuclear X-ray bursts arise from asymmetric brightness patterns on the neutron star surface and provide a direct probe of the neutron star spin frequency. We present a detailed timing analysis of the neutron star low-mass X-ray binary 4U 1323-62 using 2024 observations with the XMM-Newton and Nuclear Spectroscopic Telescope Array (NuSTAR) observatories. We identify nine thermonuclear X-ray bursts in the XMM-Newton/EPIC-pn data, along with eclipsing dips in the light curve. One of the XMM-Newton bursts exhibits a rare doublet structure. In addition, NuSTAR detects six bursts, four of which occur simultaneously with those observed with XMM-Newton. We identify a possible burst oscillation signal at ~611.5 Hz in the XMM-Newton data. The strongest oscillation, detected during the primary burst of the doublet burst, reaches a maximum <inline-formula> <mml:math><mml:msubsup><mml:mrow><mml:mi>Z</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mn>1</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msubsup></mml:math> </inline-formula> power of ~35. An analytical estimate accounting for the searched frequency and time intervals gives a significance of ~3.0σ, whereas independent Monte Carlo simulations yield a more robust global significance of only ~2.4σ. We therefore interpret the signal as a tentative detection of burst oscillation. The folded pulse profile in the 0.5--10 keV band is well described by a sinusoid, with a fractional rms amplitude of ~30% ± 6%. The oscillation frequency corresponds to a neutron star spin period of ~1.635 ms, suggesting that 4U 1323-62 may harbor a rapidly rotating millisecond neutron star.

Abstract: The objective of this proposal was to continue investigations of neutron star planetary systems in an effort to describe and understand their origin, orbital dynamics, basic physical properties and their relationship to planets around normal stars. Conclusion: 作者认为G292.0+1.8中的中子星是已识别射电脉冲星的对应体，其冷却限制略低于modified Urca过程主导的标准冷却模型；3c 58的中子星温度上限远低于标准冷却模型，暗示存在奇异粒子（如π介子凝聚）或其他提高内部中微子产生率的机制；Kes 79的致密中心源看来是该遗迹爆炸中形成的中子星。对脉冲星、磁星、单脉冲、快速射电暴（FRB）或磁星—FRB联系的相关性，根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: In the third year of this program, the following studies have been undertaken in support of this effort: G292.0+1.8: In our previous work on this SNR, we discovered a young neutron star and its associated pulsar wind nebula. Radio observations by Camilo et al. (2002) have identified a young 136 ms pulsar in the direction of G292.0+1.8. We have used Chandra HRC observations of the central source to identify X-ray pulsations at the same period, thus establishing the neutron star as the radio pulsar counterpart. We have also set limits on the cooling of this young neutron star based on the unpulsed component of the X-ray emission. We find that the limit falls slightly below standard cooling models in which the modified Urca process is responsible for the bulk of the interior neutrino emission. A paper summarizing these results is currently being circulated amongst co-authors for review prior to publication. 3c 58: Our Chandra observations of this Crab-like SNR revealed the presence of a young, rapidly rotating pulsar as well as a central compact nebula which we interpret as a toroidal structure associated with the pulsar wind termination shock. Our modeling of this structure has allowed us to establish a temperature upper limit for the neutron star which falls well below predictions from standard cooling models, and implies the presence of exotic particles (such as pion condensates) or other processes that increase the neutrino production rate in the interior. A paper summarizing this work has been published in the Astrophysical Journal (Slane, Helfand, &amp; Murray 2002, ApJ, 571, L45), and the results were the subject of a NASA Space Science Update (4/10/2002) which led to extensive media coverage. Based upon our initial observations, we submitted a successful Chandra Large Project proposal for a 350 ks observation of this young neutron star and its wind nebula. Kes 79: Our Chandra observations of this SNR reveal a compact central source which appears to be the neutron star formed in the explosion that produced the remnant. There is no evidence for a surrounding pulsar wind nebula. The source properties are similar to the central source in Cas A even though the Kes 79 remnant is considerably older. The results have been published in the Astrophysical Journal (Seward, Slane, Smith, and Sun 2003, ApJ, 584,414). Chandra Survey for Compact Objects in Supernova Remnants: We have formed a collaboration to carry out an extensive search for young neutron stars in nearby supernova remnants. Using X-ray observations from an approved Chandra Large Project, as well as from additional approved XMM observations, we are investigating a volume-limited sample of SNRs for which there is currently no evidence of associated neutron stars. We have obtained extensive optical and 1R data to complement the project, and analysis of these data is currently underway.

</details>

TL;DR: 该工作利用Breakthrough Listen对FRB121102的一次观测中在一小时内探测到21个FRB的均匀数据，比较多成分与单成分爆发的形状，并通过统计矩分析注量分布形状及人为降低信噪比，表明多成分结构可能被噪声抹平，因此多成分爆发的真实比例可能高于观测值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FRB起源未知，而已确认的重复暴为研究其信号特性提供了机会；包括FRB121102在内的重复暴存在独特的多成分爆发结构，即爆发各部分在时间上分离。理解含多成分结构的FRB真实比例，将对可能发射这些爆发的物理机制施加限制，因此需要研究该特征。

Method: 摘要指出使用FRB121102的Breakthrough Listen数据，其中一次观测在一小时内探测到21个FRB，构成均匀数据源；分析比较该数据集中多成分爆发与单成分爆发的形状，并用统计矩测量注量分布形状，还通过人为降低多成分爆发的信噪比来观察其形状相对单成分爆发的改变。摘要未说明具体望远镜、频段、时间跨度、极化或RM/DM分析等细节。

Result: 通过人为降低多成分爆发的信噪比，观察到其形状相对单成分爆发发生变化，表明多成分爆发结构可能被噪声“洗掉”，使一些FRB在观测者看来呈单成分。作者据此断言多成分爆发的真实比例可能大于观测到的比例。摘要未给出具体数值、显著性、爆发参数或统计量。

Conclusion: 作者明确支持的结论是：噪声可能把多成分爆发结构抹平，使部分FRB看似单成分，因此多成分爆发的真实比例可能高于观测值；这对限制能够发射这些爆发的物理机制具有意义。至于该形状分析方法能否推广到脉冲星、磁星或更广泛FRB群体，根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: Fast Radio Bursts (FRBs) are extremely luminous, millisecond duration radio pulses of unknown origin. While many FRBs are transient events, a few sources are confirmed to be the progenitor of multiple bursts. These "repeaters" offer an extraordinary chance to study the properties of mysterious FRB signals. The FRB121102 source was the first repeater to have ever been discovered and it has since provided us with a wealth of data to be analyzed. A unique feature of repeaters, including FRB121102, is the presence of a multicomponent burst structure. This is a phenomenon where individual portions of the burst are separated in time. Understanding the true fraction of FRBs which contain a multicomponent structure will place limitations on the physical mechanisms which can potentially emit the bursts. We investigate this feature within the Breakthrough Listen data of FRB121102. Breakthrough Listen recorded an observation that detected 21 FRBs in one hour, making it a great source of uniform data. Our analysis compares the shapes of multicomponent and single-component bursts within the Breakthrough Listen data set. We measure fluence distribution shape using statistical moments. This process is original work which provides a new method of analysis for FRB research. Through artificial reduction of the signal-to-noise ratio of multicomponent bursts, we observe how their shape changes compared to single-component bursts. This demonstrates the potential for multicomponent burst structure to be "washed out" by noise, making some FRBs appear as single-component to the observer. We assert that the true fraction of multicomponent bursts could potentially be larger than observed.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对脉冲星视线方向上的噪声信号建模可分离原始信号，从而得到更精确的脉冲周期测量，这对脉冲星计时阵列及其作为引力波背景宇宙探测器的潜力极为有用。J1614 在 B.J.Shapiro-Albert et al. (2020) 于 2014 年 3 月的一次长观测中呈现清晰、可区分的强度变化，促使进一步探究 NANOGrav 对该脉冲星的 12.5 yr 观测，以研究沿其视线方向的闪烁与星际介质。

Method: 使用 NANOGrav 12.5 yr 数据集中对毫秒脉冲星 J1614-2230 的观测。对每次观测构建动态谱，并计算二维自相关函数（2D ACF），由此得到闪烁参数；闪烁参数为描述动态谱中平均闪烁元特征的三个变量：宽度、高度和旋转。通过构建这些参数的时间序列来探究 12.5 yr 观测期间的趋势，并尝试正弦曲线拟合。散射介质的量使用推导自 J.M.Cordes &amp; B.J.Rickett (1998) 的薄屏模型计算，并采用 H II 区（Sh 2-7）的已知测量（S.K.Ocker et al. (2020)）进行近似。观测频率或频段、 telescopes 或仪器名称以及时间跨度细节摘要未说明。

Result: 闪烁参数时间序列中，正弦曲线拟合对其中一个参数而言较为成功，而另外两个参数需要更多数据点以及对 2D ACF 更精确的约束来重新审视。对于宽度变化，正弦拟合未成功，但提出了一个尝试性模型来解释所观测到的趋势。计算了散射介质强度、单位长度均方散射角以及折射角的取值。进一步利用折射角信息估算了由折射引起的脉冲延迟的尺度。

Conclusion: 作者认为，对脉冲星视线方向噪声信号建模以分离原始信号可获得更精确的脉冲周期测量，这对脉冲星计时阵列及其作为引力波背景宇宙探测器的潜力极为有用；估算折射所致脉冲延迟尺度将对精确定位由引力波背景引起的延迟至关重要，而这是 NANOGrav 的一个主要目标。宽度变化的尝试性模型仅为对观测趋势的试探性解释，摘要未给出其物理机制细节。

Abstract: Observations of the millisecond pulsar J1614-2230 as part of the NANOGrav 12.5 yr dataset were utilised to study the scintillation and interstellar medium along the line of sight of PSR J1614-2230. Modelling noise signals in pulsar line of sight allows for isolation of the raw signal and therefore more precise pulse period measurements. These are extremely useful in Pulsar Timing Arrays and in their potential to be used as cosmic detectors for the gravitational wave background. J1614 presented clear, distinguishable intensity variations in a long observation taken in March 2014 by B.J.Shapiro-Albert et al. (2020), which provided motivation to further probe NANOGrav’s 12.5 yr observations of the pulsar. Dynamic spectra were constructed for each observation, from which the 2D Auto correlation function and consequently, the scintillation parameters, were calculated. Scintillation parameters consist of three variables describing the characteristic nature of the average scintle in a dynamic spectrum: the width, height and rotation. The construction of time series of these parameters allowed for probing of trends across the 12.5 yr observations. Sinusoidal curve fitting was reasonably successful for one of these parameters while the other two need to be revisited with more data points and more precise constraints on the 2D ACF. For the width variation, although a sinusoidal fit was unsuccessful, a tentative model to explain the observed trend is proposed. The scintillation parameters are also tied to descriptions of the scattering medium, namely the strength of the scattering medium, the mean-square scattering angle per unit length and the refractive angle. Values of each of these were calculated using the thin screen model, derived in J.M.Cordes &amp; B.J.Rickett (1998). Known measurements (S.K.Ocker et al. (2020)) of the H II region eclipsing the pulsar (Sh 2-7) were also used in approximating these values. Information about the refractive angle was further used to estimate a scale for the pulse delay caused by refraction, which will be essential in pinpointing delays due to the gravitational wave background, a main goal of NANOGrav.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚脉冲漂移脉冲星通常表现出占主导的漂移发射，但少数情况下会出现罕见的非漂移状态。该文旨在通过单脉冲观测刻画 PSR J1059-5742 的发射状态组成、漂移特征以及漂移与零脉冲之间的关系，理解其发射状态变化所反映的极冠过程。

Abstract: We report single-pulse observation of PSR J1059-5742 carried out with the 64-m Parkes radio telescope. Single-pulse analysis of the observation identifies three distinct emission states: a dominant subpulse-drifting state, a rare brighter non-drifting state, and short nulls. Our study show that 96.2% of the pulsar's radio radiation is in the drifting state. In the drifting state, subpulses drift toward earlier longitudes, with drift periods of <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>P</mml:mi><mml:mn>3</mml:mn></mml:msub><mml:mo>=</mml:mo><mml:mn>5.7</mml:mn><mml:mo>±</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.5</mml:mn><mml:mi>P</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula> and <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>P</mml:mi><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:msub><mml:mo>=</mml:mo><mml:mn>5.6</mml:mn><mml:mo>±</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mn>0.7</mml:mn><mml:mo>∘</mml:mo></mml:msup></mml:math></inline-formula>. In the observation, we detect two rare non-drifting state events. The two non-drifting-state events persist for 107 and 108 rotation periods respectively. These two events accounted for 3.8% of the total observing time. Compared with single pulses in the drifting state, those in the non-drifting state are brighter, and the peak of the average pulse profile for the non-drifting state leads that of the drifting state by about <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msup><mml:mn>2</mml:mn><mml:mo>∘</mml:mo></mml:msup></mml:math></inline-formula> in spin longitude. This implies that changes may have occurred in the polar-cap discharge process or the magnetospheric configuration. Examination of the energies of all detected single-pulses revealed that the pulsar may have a nulling fraction of <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mn>13</mml:mn><mml:mo>±</mml:mo><mml:mn>1</mml:mn><mml:mi>%</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula>, and pulse nulling occurs only within the drifting state. The occasional cessation of drifting in PSR J1059-5742 may thus reveal a mode change of the polar-cap discharge.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长周期脉冲星J1840-0840的复杂辐射行为尚需系统性观测研究，尤其是其脉冲零化与子脉冲漂移性质，以及这些性质在不同观测频率下如何变化。

Result: 该脉冲星平均脉冲轮廓包含两个明显且空间分离的辐射成分，其频率演化与经典半径-频率关系预言一致，表明射电发射高度随观测频率增加而系统性降低。单脉冲分析揭示显著脉冲零化与子脉冲漂移；625 MHz与1358 MHz零化比例分别为32.63%±10.46%和28.36%±7.35%，脉冲爆发态与零化态发生率均随持续时间增加呈指数衰减。观测到准周期零化：625 MHz处两个调制周期为自转周期的(50.57±9.03)倍与(21.33±3.91)倍，1358 MHz处一个调制周期为(31.37±11.90)倍。各漂移带建模给出相位间隔、漂移周期与漂移率随时间的变化，结果显示子脉冲漂移参数具有显著频率依赖性。

Abstract: This study presents a systematic investigation of the complex radiation behavior of the long-period pulsar J1840-0840 based on dual-frequency observations conducted with the Giant Metrewave Radio Telescope (GMRT) in India and the 100-meter Effelsberg radio telescope in Germany. The average pulse profile of this pulsar exhibits two distinct and spatially separated radiation components. The frequency evolution of the average profile is consistent with the theoretical predictions of the classical radius-frequency relation, indicating that the radio emission height of the pulsar systematically decreases with increasing observation frequency. Single-pulse analysis reveals significant pulse nulling and subpulse drifting characteristics in this pulsar. The nulling fractions at 625 MHz and 1358 MHz are 32.63%<mml:math><mml:mo>±</mml:mo></mml:math>10.46% and 28.36%<mml:math><mml:mo>±</mml:mo></mml:math>7.35%, respectively. The occurrence rates of both pulse burst and nulling states exhibit an exponential decay with increasing duration. Additionally, PSR J1840-0840 shows quasi-periodic nulling behavior, with two modulation periods at 625 MHz, namely (50.57<mml:math><mml:mo>±</mml:mo></mml:math>9.03) and (21.33<mml:math><mml:mo>±</mml:mo></mml:math>3.91) times the spin period, and one modulation period at 1358 MHz, which is (31.37<mml:math><mml:mo>±</mml:mo></mml:math>11.90) times the spin period. By modeling each drifting band, the phase separation, drift period, and drift rate evolution over time were obtained. The results indicate a significant frequency dependence of the subpulse drifting parameters.

</details>

Abstract: Vela.jl performs Bayesian pulsar timing and noise analysis. It supports narrowband and wideband TOAs along with most commonly used pulsar timing models. The code provides an independent, efficient, and parallelized implementation of the full nonlinear pulsar timing and noise model and includes a Python binding (pyvela). One-time operations such as data file input, clock corrections, and solar system ephemeris computations are performed by pyvela with the help of the PINT (ascl:1902.007) pulsar timing package.

### [35] [Relativistic Transients from Compact Objects: Radiation Processes and Geometric Effects]()
*Chen, Connery*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 该研究发展理论与计算框架，量化几何和相对论运动对来自快速射电暴、伽马射线暴和中子星并合等致密天体相对论性暂现源辐射的影响，并表明几何效应与辐射过程在解读暂现辐射时不可分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 致密天体产生的相对论性暂现源处于强引力、超相对论外流与高能辐射同时起作用的极端物理 regime；为了正确解读观测并约束源性质，需要理解其辐射机制以及外禀的几何和相对论效应。摘要指出，仅靠几何无法解释某些快速 X 射线暂现源的能量，因此需理解其辐射机制、外流动力学与前身星性质。

Method: 摘要说明作者发展理论和计算框架，用以量化几何和相对论运动如何塑造 FRB、GRB 和中子星并合的观测辐射；分析银河系磁星 SGR J1935+2154 的 FRB；提出并应用 PromptX 工具包模拟中子星并合的 X 射线对应体，计算观测者系光变曲线和能谱；并与 VegasAfterglow 等 GRB 余辉建模工具包配合。具体观测频率、时间跨度与样本量摘要未说明。

Result: 对银河系磁星 SGR J1935+2154 的 FRB 分析表明，辐射几何强烈主导爆发能量和探测概率，并影响射电与 X 射线辐射之间的联系。PromptX 能计算观测者系光变曲线和能谱，以快速表征多信使事件；与 VegasAfterglow 等工具包一起提供预测性、低延迟诊断以优化宽带后随观测。将 PromptX 应用于 Einstein Probe 探测到的不断增多的明亮、能谱偏软的快速 X 射线暂现源群体，结果显示仅几何无法解释其观测到的能量。

Conclusion: 作者得出结论：在解读来自致密天体的暂现辐射时，几何效应与辐射过程不可分割，解读这些快速 X 射线暂现源需要理解驱动它们的独特辐射机制、外流动力学和前身星性质。所提出的框架将理论模型与高能天体物理观测相结合，是自洽的建模框架。对于该框架向脉冲星单脉冲、脉冲轮廓演化或脉冲星计时等具体课题的迁移性，根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: Relativistic transients from compact objects probe the most extreme regimes of physics, where strong gravity, ultra-relativistic outflows, and high-energy radiation operate simultaneously; understanding the underlying radiation mechanisms and extrinsic geometric and relativistic effects is essential for interpreting observations and constraining source properties. I develop theoretical and computational frameworks to quantify how geometry and relativistic motion shape observed emission from fast radio bursts, gamma-ray bursts, and neutron star mergers. I analyze fast radio bursts from the Galactic magnetar SGR J1935+2154 and show that emission geometry strongly governs burst energetics and detection probability, and influences the link between radio and X-ray emission. I present PromptX, a computational toolkit for modeling X-ray counterparts to neutron star mergers. PromptX computes observer-frame light curves and spectra, enabling rapid characterization of multimessenger events. In tandem with GRB afterglow modeling toolkits such as VegasAfterglow, these frameworks provide predictive, low-latency diagnostics to inform and optimize broadband follow-up campaigns. Applying PromptX to the growing population of luminous, spectrally soft fast X-ray transients detected by the Einstein Probe, I show that geometry alone cannot explain their observed energetics. Interpreting these events requires understanding the distinct radiation mechanisms, outflow dynamics, and progenitor properties that power them. These results demonstrate that geometric effects and radiation processes are inseparable in interpreting transient emission from compact objects and provide self-consistent modeling frameworks that integrate theoretical models with high-energy astrophysical observations.

</details>

Abstract: The grant provided funds for a conference entitled 'Neutron Stars in Supernova Remnants' held in Boston on 14-17 August 2001, in part to support invited speakers and students attending the meeting. The conference included talks and papers on the most recent work in this field, including results from the Chandra X-ray Observatory, XMM-Newton, the Parkes Multibeam Pulsar Survey, the Very Large Array, and many other facilities. Theoretical work based on the latest results was also highlighted. The Proceedings of the conference have now been published as 'Neutron Stars in Supernova Remnants'. In addition, a large fraction of the papers from the conference have been submitted to astro-ph, and the volume in indexed through the Astronomical Data System.

### [38] [The first NenuFAR pulsar catalogue]()
*Bondonneau, L.,Grießmeier, J.-M.,Theureau, G.,Cognard, I.,Jankowski, F.,Kondratiev, V.,Kravtsov, I.,Pétri, J.,Possenti, A.,Ulyanov, O.,Zakharenko, V.,Brionne, M.,Cecconi, B.,Corbel, S.,Girard, J. N.,Konovalenko, O.,Tokarsky, P.,Zarka, P.*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: NenuFAR 在 10--85 MHz 对北天已知脉冲星的首次低频普查探测到 182 颗脉冲星，其中 89 颗此前从未在此频段被探测，并测量了新的 DM、散射指数、占空比与校准通量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低于 100 MHz 的射电频率范围此前在扩展的脉冲星族群研究中尚未被探索。该计划是未来脉冲星关键科学项目的第一步，旨在为针对特殊兴趣脉冲星的研究、探索预期翻转点以下的脉冲星频谱与脉冲形状演化，以及研究星际和日球层等离子体引起的色散与散射做准备。

Method: 使用 NenuFAR（10--85 MHz）在 2019--2021 年调试与早期科学阶段观测从 Nançay 射电天文台可观测的所有已知脉冲星，即赤纬高于 -20° 且 DM 最高达 100 pc cm<SUP>-3</SUP>；样本选择时 ATNF 脉冲星表中有 576 颗满足条件，另加 69 颗 LOTAAS/LOFAR 脉冲星，共 645 颗。使用 NenuFAR 核心中 56 个迷你阵列（1064 个天线，约 58% 标称收集面积），观测时长为 25 至 180 分钟，取决于高度和性质。

Result: 在 10--85 MHz 内探测到 182 颗脉冲星，其中 89 颗此前从未在该范围被看到；探测到 13 颗毫秒脉冲星，其中十颗是 100 MHz 以下的新探测。为所有脉冲星测量了新的 DM、散射指数、占空比和校准通量。MSPs 的典型最小可探测脉冲为 1--2 mJy，常规族群为 5--10 mJy。发布了所有目标的全部频段相位图，并在脉冲星足够亮时给出频率分辨的脉冲轮廓。

Conclusion: 该普查是未来脉冲星关键科学项目的第一个垫脚石，旨在为模式转换、漂移子脉冲和偏振辐射等特殊兴趣脉冲星的定向研究做准备，探索预期翻转点以下的脉冲星频谱与脉冲形状演化，并研究星际和日球层等离子体引起的色散与散射。根据摘要无法判断其结果对磁星、FRB、计时或自旋下降力矩变化的直接意义。

Abstract: Context. The radio frequency range below 100 MHz has been unexplored until recently for extended pulsar population studies. We present the first low-frequency pulsar census of the northern sky with the NenuFAR radio telescope (10--85 MHz), performed during its commissioning and early science phase in 2019--2021. Aims. Our programme was conceived as the first stepping stone of a future pulsar key science project, aimed at preparing for targeted studies on pulsars of peculiar interest (mode switching, drifting subpulses, and polarised emission), exploring pulsar spectra and pulse-shape evolution below the expected turn-over, and investigating dispersion and scattering due to the interstellar and heliospheric plasma. Methods. We observed all known pulsars observable from the Nançay Radio Observatory in France, i.e. with a declination above -20° and dispersion measures (DMs) up to 100 pc cm<SUP>-3</SUP>. At the time of the sample selection, 576 pulsars fulfilled these criteria in the ATNF pulsar catalogue. We complemented this sample with 69 additional LOTAAS/LOFAR pulsars, yielding 645 observed pulsars. We used the 56 mini-arrays available in the NenuFAR core (i.e. 1064 antennas), corresponding to ~58% of the nominal collecting area. Targets were observed for between 25 and 180 minutes, depending on their elevation and nature (regular or millisecond pulsars). Results. We detected 182 pulsars in the frequency range 10--85 MHz, 89 of which had never been seen before in this range. We detected 13 millisecond pulsars, ten of which are new detections below 100 MHz. We measured new DMs, scattering indices, duty cycles, and calibrated fluxes for all of them. The typical minimum detectable pulse was 1--2 mJy for MSPs and 5--10 mJy for the regular population. We release full-band phasograms for all targets, with frequency-resolved pulse profiles where the pulsar was bright enough.

</details>


### [39] [High-resolution measurements with the CTAO southern array: The case for pulsar wind nebulae]()
*Schwefer, G.,Hinton, J. A.*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 以HESS J1813-178和MSH 15-52为例的模拟研究表明，CTAO南阵列在multi-TeV能量处的未来观测结合已有X射线测量，很可能能够约束这些脉冲星风云中的磁场与高能电子分布；改进的角分辨率可显著增强灵敏度，但在相关multi-TeV regime，信号光子统计量仍是限制，以事件统计量换取角分辨率并非必然有利。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CTAO的出现及切伦科夫望远镜伽马射线光子重建的进展，预计将把伽马射线波段角分辨率极限推进到数十TeV处小于一角分，从而开启新的科学研究可能。作者希望表明脉冲星风云（PWNe）研究尤其会受益于这些高分辨率测量，因为PWNe是TeV能量处占主导的银河系源族群，具有延伸至数百TeV的硬谱，并且从X射线观测已知在角分尺度上具有丰富结构。

Method: 以HESS J1813-178和MSH 15-52为例，基于X射线观测和已有伽马射线测量构建这些源TeV形态的简单轻子模型。随后假设天文台曝光和点扩展函数的不同模型，用未来的CTAO南阵列创建模拟观测，并据此评估这些观测区分模型、研究这些源物理（特别是推断磁场和电子分布结构）的能力。摘要未说明具体观测频段、时间跨度或样本量。

Result: 作者发现，未来用CTAO南阵列在multi-TeV能量处的观测——结合已有X射线测量——很可能能够约束这些源中的磁场和高能电子分布。他们证明，借助新颖重建算法可实现的改进角分辨率，这些测量的灵敏度能够显著增强。然而，他们也表明，在相关的multi-TeV regime，信号光子统计量仍然是一个限制，并且用事件统计量换取改进的角分辨率并不必然有利。摘要未给出具体数值、误差或统计显著性。

Conclusion: 作者明确支持的解释是：CTAO南阵列未来的multi-TeV观测与已有X射线测量结合，很可能能够约束所研究脉冲星风云中的磁场和高能电子分布，且新颖重建算法带来的角分辨率改进可显著增强测量灵敏度；但在相关multi-TeV regime，信号光子统计量仍是限制，以事件统计量换取角分辨率并非必然有利。该研究针对脉冲星风云，而非单脉冲、模式转换、脉冲星计时、自转减慢力矩变化、偏振、射电谱或磁星—快速射电暴（FRB）联系；其对这些主题的可迁移性根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: Context. The advent of the Cherenkov Telescope Array Observatory (CTAO) and recent advances in reconstruction of gamma-ray photons with Cherenkov telescopes are bound to push the limit of angular resolution to an unprecedented level of precision (in the gamma-ray band) of less than one arcminute at tens of Teraelectronvolts. Naturally, such instrumental improvements open up possibilities for new and interesting scientific studies. Aims. We aim to show that the study of pulsar wind nebulae (PWNe) in particular is bound to benefit from these high-resolution measurements. This is because PWNe are the dominant Galactic source population at TeV energies, exhibit hard spectra up to hundreds of TeV, and are known to possess plentiful structure on arcminute scales from X-ray observations. Methods. Using HESS J1813-178 and MSH 15-52 as examples, we created simple leptonic models of the TeV morphology of these sources based on X-ray observations and existing gamma-ray measurements. Then, assuming different models for the exposure and point spread function of the observatory, we created mock observations with the future CTAO southern array. We used these to assess the ability of these observations to differentiate between models and study the physics of these sources, in particular to infer the structure of the magnetic field and electron distributions. Results. We find that future observations with the CTAO southern array at multi-TeV energies -- in combination with existing X-ray measurements -- will likely be able to constrain the distributions of magnetic field and high-energy electrons in these sources. We demonstrate that the sensitivity of these measurements can be significantly enhanced with the improved angular resolution achievable with novel reconstruction algorithms. However, we also show that in the relevant multi-TeV regime, signal-photon statistics remain a limitation and that trading event statistics for improved angular resolution is not necessarily advantageous.

</details>

Abstract: We test whether the Efron-Petrosian (E-P) method can recover the inverse-square law dependence of the radio pulsar flux, using a synthetic catalog generated according to the specifications of the Parkes Multi-beam survey using the PsrPopPy software. We find that the E-P method cannot automatically reproduce the inverse-square scaling law for the radio pulsar flux, even though this scaling is built into the synthetic data by construction, except over a narrow range of flux thresholds, and even here we don't get pristine agreement. The main reason for the deviation is that the synthetic radio pulsar catalog is truncated based on a cut on the pulsar signal to noise ratio (SNR), which has a non-linear dependence on the flux along with considerable scatter. We show that the disagreement is exacerbated as we raise the SNR threshold. We then demonstrate that if we create a synthetic catalog based on a flux cut (instead of an SNR-based threshold), we can recover the true distance exponent, with an accuracy ranging from pristine agreement to within ± 1σ depending on the chosen flux threshold. Therefore, the E-P method cannot be used to determine the correct scaling of radio pulsar flux with distance. Our results also demonstrate that the E-P method breaks down when the detection threshold scales non-linearly with flux along with scatter.

</details>

Result: 发现零脉冲脉冲星 PSR J0052--7551，其自转周期为 0.55 s，周期导数为 6.1(6) $\times 10^{-17}$ s s$^{-1}$，DM 为 29.24(2) pc cm$^{-3}$，在投影上位于 SMC 外围。高斯混合模型拟合得到零脉冲比例约为 $\sim 32$%，零脉冲长度与指数分布一致，表明为随机过程。UHF 可见度数据的功率谱显示约 $\sim$5--7 s 的准周期特征无法解释，且不能归因于源本身、射频干扰或标准仪器效应。TRON 由典型脉冲星零脉冲行为相关的约分钟时标变化触发。

Conclusion: 作者认为，这是继此前依赖闪烁或食行为的 TRON 图像平面脉冲星探测之后，首次由零脉冲触发的探测，证实了 TRON 对中等时标持续时间的广泛天体物理现象的敏感性。零脉冲长度呈指数分布，指示随机过程；约 $\sim$5--7 s 准周期特征的来源根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: We report on the discovery of a nulling pulsar, PSR J0052--7551, in archival MeerKAT L-band observations of the Small Magellanic Cloud (SMC). The discovery was made using the image-plane transient detection pipeline, TRON, which creates a residual image for each time sample and uses a heuristic pruning strategy to identify promising candidates in the resulting temporal image cube. The TRON pipeline was triggered on the approximately minute-time-scale variability associated with the nulling behaviour of a canonical pulsar. Follow-up MeerKAT UHF-band beamformer observations revealed a pulsar with a spin period of 0.55 s and a measured period derivative of 6.1(6) <inline-formula><tex-math>$\times 10^{-17}$</tex-math></inline-formula> s s<inline-formula><tex-math>$^{-1}$</tex-math></inline-formula>. The source is seen in projection towards the periphery of the SMC, and has a dispersion measure value of 29.24(2) pc cm<inline-formula><tex-math>$^{-3}$</tex-math></inline-formula>, which is on the order of the expected Milky Way contribution along the line of sight. We use a Gaussian Mixture Model to fit the nulling behaviour, which yielded a nulling fraction of <inline-formula><tex-math>$\sim 32$</tex-math></inline-formula>%. We find that the null lengths are consistent with an exponential distribution, indicating a stochastic process. Additionally, power spectra of the UHF visibility data reveal an unexplained quasi-periodic feature at <inline-formula><tex-math>$\sim$</tex-math></inline-formula>5--7 s that cannot be attributed to the source itself, radio frequency interference, or standard instrumental effects. Following on from previous image-plane detections of pulsars by the TRON approach, which relied on either scintillation or eclipsing behaviour, this is the first detection triggered by nulling. This discovery confirms TRON's sensitivity to a wide range of astrophysical phenomena of medium time-scale duration.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 使用 LHAASO 的 WCDA（0.4--15 TeV）和 KM2A（&gt; 25 TeV）观测 DA 495；分析基于 Chandra 和 XMM-Newton 的 X 射线数据；进行 Fermi-LAT 光谱分析；用覆盖射电、X 射线和 TeV γ 射线波段的宽波段谱能量分布拟合单区轻子模型；并采用一个粒子输运在内区（约 100″ 以内）由对流主导、外区由扩散主导的时变模型。

Result: LHAASO 观测揭示能量相关形态：WCDA（0.4--15 TeV）探测到 r<SUB>39</SUB> = 0.19° ± 0.02° 的延展源，KM2A（&gt; 25 TeV）观测到点状源，其 r<SUB>39</SUB> 的 95% 置信上限为 0.11°。光谱延伸至 100 TeV 以上，并在数十 TeV 处存在折断或截断。X 射线数据分析显示 DA 495 的 X 射线辐射延伸至约 6'，显著大于此前报道的尺寸。宽波段谱能量分布拟合给出平均磁场约 5 µG，Fermi-LAT 光谱分析表明系统中可能存在一颗 γ 射线脉冲星。时变模型成功再现了 X 射线表面亮度和谱指数的径向轮廓，并解释了 LHAASO 探测到的 TeV γ 射线辐射。

Conclusion: 作者认为 DA 495 是一个演化了的脉冲星风云（PWN），其中持续的粒子扩散产生了 TeV 晕成分，因此它是一个 PWN+晕系统；内区对流主导、外区扩散主导的时变模型支持这一解释。摘要提及 Fermi-LAT 表明系统中可能存在 γ 射线脉冲星，但未说明与快速射电暴、单脉冲发射或模式转换的直接联系。

Abstract: Pulsar wind nebula DA 495 (G65.7+1.2) has been extensively observed from radio to TeV γ-ray bands. We present LHAASO observations of DA 495, revealing an energy-dependent morphology, where an extended source with r<SUB>39</SUB> = 0.19° ± 0.02° is detected by WCDA (0.4--15 TeV), and a point-like source with a 95% upper limit of r<SUB>39</SUB> = 0.11° is observed by KM2A (&gt; 25 TeV). The spectrum of the source extends beyond 100 TeV with a break or cutoff at a few tens of TeV. Our X-ray data analysis, based on Chandra and XMM-Newton observations, shows that the X-ray emission of DA 495 extends well to ~6', significantly larger than the size previously reported. The broadband spectral energy distribution across radio, X-ray, and TeV γ-ray bands is described by a one-zone leptonic model, yielding an average magnetic field of ~5 µG, while Fermi-LAT spectral analysis indicates a likely presence of a γ-ray pulsar within the system. A time-dependent model, in which particle transport is convection-dominated in the inner region (within ~100″) and diffusion-dominated in the outer region, successfully reproduces the observed radial profiles of X-ray surface brightness and spectral index, and also accounts for the TeV γ-ray emission detected by LHAASO, suggesting that DA 495 represents an evolved PWN with ongoing particle diffusion that gives rise to a TeV halo component---that is, a PWN+halo system.

</details>

TL;DR: 对由PSR J1740+1000驱动的弓激波脉冲星风云，EP与LHAASO联合观测首次发现与空间重合的延伸UHE γ射线发射相伴的延伸X射线尾，表明高于100 TeV的电子/正电子在至少~10 pc尺度上各向异性地从PWN逃逸，而非立即各向同性扩散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲星风云是主要宇宙线加速器，但高能粒子如何被输运进入星际介质仍不清楚。此前LHAASO在由PSR J1740+1000驱动的弓激波脉冲星风云附近发现了一个超高能γ射线源，为研究该输运过程提供了目标。摘要指出，通常假设粒子立即各向同性地扩散进入周围星际介质，而该系统的粒子输运方式尚待揭示。

Method: 基于LHAASO对由脉冲星PSR J1740+1000驱动的弓激波PWN附近UHE γ射线源的发现，开展Einstein Probe（EP）与LHAASO的联合研究。EP观测用于揭示X射线结构，并与先前XMM-Newton所见结构比较；更新后的LHAASO观测用于刻画γ射线发射的形态，并与EP的X射线尾进行空间对齐分析。摘要还提到用同步辐射和逆康普顿辐射对X射线与γ射线能谱进行解释。摘要未说明具体观测频率或能段、时间跨度、样本量和统计方法。

Result: EP观测揭示出一条延伸X射线尾，远远超过此前XMM-Newton看到的结构。更新后的LHAASO观测显示γ射线发射呈拉长形态，其长轴与EP揭示的延伸X射线尾对齐。摘要称这是首次探测到与空间上重合的延伸UHE γ射线发射相关的X射线脉冲星尾。X射线和γ射线能谱可由单一相对论电子族群分别通过同步辐射和逆康普顿辐射很好地解释。结果明确表明高于100 TeV的电子/正电子正在从PWN中逃逸，并在至少~10 pc尺度上各向异性地输运。

Conclusion: 作者认为，X射线与γ射线能谱可由单一相对论电子族群通过同步辐射和逆康普顿辐射解释，因此无需在传播过程中引入粒子再加速。高于100 TeV的电子/正电子正在从PWN逃逸；它们并非立即各向同性地扩散进入周围星际介质，而是在至少~10 pc尺度上各向异性地输运，可能由背景磁场引导，也可能由平流外流携带。就脉冲星、磁星、单脉冲、脉冲轮廓、模式转换、计时、偏振或FRB等主题的关联而言，摘要未说明；根据摘要无法判断其可迁移性。

Abstract: Pulsar wind nebulae (PWNe) are major cosmic ray accelerators, yet the mechanisms transporting high-energy particles into the interstellar medium remain elusive. Building on the LHAASO discovery of an ultra-high-energy (UHE) γ-ray source near the bow-shock PWN powered by the pulsar PSR J1740+1000, we present a joint Einstein Probe (EP) and LHAASO study of this system. EP observations reveal an extended X-ray tail far exceeding the structure previously seen by XMM-Newton. Updated LHAASO observations show that the γ-ray emission is elongated, with its major axis aligned with the extended X-ray tail revealed by EP. This is the first detection of an X-ray pulsar tail associated with a spatially coincident extended UHE γ-ray emission. The X-ray and γ-ray spectrum can be well explained with a single population of relativistic electrons via synchrotron and inverse Compton radiation, respectively, removing the need for particle re-acceleration during propagation. The results unambiguously show that electrons/positrons above 100 TeV are escaping from the PWN. Instead of the immediate, isotropic diffusion into ambient interstellar medium that is typically assumed, these particles are transported anisotropically over at least ~10 pc, either guided by the background magnetic field or carried by an advective outflow.

</details>


### [46] [A Search of M31 for Giant Radio Pulses from Radio Pulsars and Magnetars]()
*Saroff, David*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 使用 GBT 在 300–400 MHz 对 M31 进行 40 小时巨射电脉冲巡天，未探测到规律重复的脉冲，但发现了若干 DM 对 M31 合理的候选孤立或少量重复事件，其中包括一个 DM 为 54.7 pc cm−3 的候选事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对近邻星系 M31 中射电脉冲星和磁星的巨射电脉冲（GRP）开展搜寻。摘要指出，银河系或麦哲伦云中已知脉冲星的 GRP 若移到 M31 将无法用 GBT 在该频段探测到，而 M31 中最亮 GRP 仅处于可探测性 2 倍因子之内，这构成该项巡天的探测能力背景。

Method: 使用绿岸射电望远镜（GBT）在 300 MHz 至 400 MHz 频段对 M31 进行 40 小时巡天，覆盖 M31 距离上 8000 kpc2 投影面积，包括银河系核球和大部分恒星盘，包含 M32，不含 M110，部分采样矮星系 AND III。观测为 2.3 分钟单次指向。分析了 2015 年猎户座流星雨和金牛座流星雨期间空中交通管制信道经流星头散射进入波束的干扰，并报告干扰缓解及数据可视化方法。使用 PRESTO 库搜索 30 pc cm−3 至 500 pc cm−3 的 DM。

Result: 观测覆盖 M31 距离上 8000 kpc2 投影面积。在 2.3 分钟单次指向期间没有探测到规律重复的脉冲，但探测到候选事件，这些事件在时间上孤立或有一到两次重复，其 DM 对 M31 合理。探测到一个 DM 为 54.7 pc cm−3 的候选事件，该值与 [Rubio-Herrera et al., 2013] 报告的 6 个事件的值相同。观测结果与使用 LOFAR 对 M31 的脉冲搜索一致。摘要未说明探测的统计显著性。

Conclusion: 作者指出，如果任何候选事件是真实事件，那么 M31 中必定存在一类比蟹状星云脉冲星更明亮、并且间歇性发射极明亮脉冲的天体群体。观测结果与使用 LOFAR 对 M31 的脉冲搜索一致。根据摘要无法判断这些候选事件与磁星、快速射电暴（FRB）、脉冲轮廓演化或自转减速转矩变化之间的具体物理联系。

Abstract: We present results of a 40-hour survey of the nearby galaxy M31 for giant radio pulses (GRPs) from radio pulsars using the Green Bank Radio telescope (GBT) in the 300 MHz to 400 MHz band. We note that GRPs from known pulsars in the Milky Way or its nearest companion galaxies, the Magellanic clouds, if relocated to M31, would not be detectable with the GBT in the 300 MHz to 400 MHz band. However, the brightest GRPs from a pulsar in M31, like the Crab pulsar B0531+21, are within a factor of 2 of detectability. Our observations cover a projected surface area of 8000 kpc2 at the distance of M31 including the Galactic bulge and most of the stellar disk. M32 is included, but M110 is not. The dwarf galaxy AND III is partially sampled. We analyze sources of interference including meteor head scattering of military and civilian air traffic control channels into the GBT beam during the 2015 Orionid and Taurid meteor showers coincident with our observing time. We report our methods for interference mitigation and methods for visualizing and exploring the data. Dispersion measures (DM) from 30 pc cm−3 to 500 pc cm−3 were searched using the PRESTO library. There are no detections of pulses which repeated regularly during our 2.3-minute individual pointings. However, there are detections of candidate events which are either isolated in time or have one or two repetitions during our observations. These candidates have dispersion measures which are plausible values for M31. We detect a candidate event which has a dispersion measure of 54.7 pc cm−3 which is the value of the 6 events reported by [Rubio-Herrera et al., 2013]. Our observations are consistent with a search for pulses in M31 with LOFAR. If any of our candidates are real events, there must be a population of objects in M31 which are more luminous than the Crab pulsar and which emit very luminous pulses intermittently.

</details>


### [47] [Dependences of radio pulsar parameters on the kick velocity]()
*Lazarev, Anton D.,Popov, Sergei B.*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 利用约 200 颗正常孤立射电脉冲星的数据检验中子星诞生踢速度的双峰分布提议，发现约 23% 属于低速度模式，但物理参数中不存在与踢速度双峰性相关的稳健双峰性，这被视为反对该双峰性的间接论据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 若干研究通过观测数据分析与理论建模倾向于支持中子星诞生速度踢呈双峰分布，本文旨在利用射电脉冲星数据检验这一提议是否在脉冲星物理参数中留下可辨识的对应特征。

Method: 摘要未说明所用望远镜、观测频段与时间跨度。研究使用约 200 颗具有良好测量的自转和运动学参数的正常孤立射电脉冲星，应用 Igoshev (2020) 提出的参数化方法判定其属于低速度模式还是高速度模式，并比较两组脉冲星的性质，涵盖特征年龄、距离、脉冲宽度与磁场分布，并讨论特征年龄上限等选择效应的影响。

Result: 约 23% 的样本属于低速度模式。特征年龄和距离在两种模式间存在明显差异，但可归因于选择偏差；脉冲宽度等不受强烈选择影响的参数未见差异。两种模式的磁场分布存在显著差异：低场脉冲星（\(B\lesssim10^{12}~\)G）在低速度模式中占比过高，而在低场脉冲星（\(\lesssim10^{11}~\)G）中未识别出任何高速度模式天体。该差异的起源尚不清楚，且当引入特征年龄上限时，磁场分布差异表现出复杂行为。

Conclusion: 作者认为磁场分布差异很可能可由选择效应解释，因此射电脉冲星的物理参数中不存在能够与所提出的踢速度双峰性相关联的稳健双峰性；这可以作为反对中子星踢分布中假设双峰性的间接论据。根据摘要无法判断该结论对磁星、快速射电暴或其他瞬变射电现象的适用性。

Abstract: According to several studies, analysis of observational data and theoretical modeling favor a bimodal distribution of the natal velocity kick of neutron stars. We analyze this proposal by using available data on radio pulsars. For ~200 normal isolated radio pulsars with well-measured spin and kinematic parameters, we determine if they belong to the low- or high-velocity mode of such a distribution by applying the parametrization proposed by Igoshev (2020). Our results demonstrate that about 23% belong to the low-velocity mode. We then analyze the differences in the properties of the two sets of pulsars belonging to the two modes. For some parameters (characteristic ages and distances), we see a clear difference between the two modes. However, for these quantities, it can be easily attributed to selection bias. For those parameters that are not a subject of strong selection, such as pulse width, we do not observe any difference. Interestingly, we detect a notable difference in the magnetic field distribution between the two modes. Lower field pulsars ( B≲1012~G) are overabundant among objects from the low-velocity mode in comparison to the high-velocity one. In particular, among pulsars with low fields ( ≲1011~G), we do not identify any objects from the high-velocity mode of the kick distribution. The origin of this discrepancy is not clear, and we discuss several possibilities. Our analysis demonstrates that most probably, this feature can be explained by selection effects. In particular, the difference in the magnetic field distribution for low- and high-velocity pulsars demonstrates complicated behavior when an upper limit on the characteristic age of pulsars is introduced. Thus, we conclude that there is no robust bimodality in physical parameters of radio pulsars that can be related to the proposed bimodality of the kick velocity. This can be considered as an indirect argument against the hypothetical bimodality in the NS kick distribution.

</details>


### [48] [Disentangling X-Ray Emission Processes In Vela-Like Pulsars]()
*Gaensler, Bryan,Mushotzky, Richard*

TL;DR: 对年轻中子星B1823-13的XMM观测数据分析表明，其中心核的未脉冲化延展非热辐射来自脉冲星驱动的同步辐射星云，而温度过高的弥散延展成分被解释为周围超新星遗迹壳层的非热辐射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两颗年轻中子星B1823-13和B1046-58的X射线辐射预计由超新星遗迹、风驱动同步辐射星云、磁层脉动和中子星表面等多种过程复杂混合而成。要分离并分别研究这些过程，需要XMM在光谱、时间和角分辨率上的独特组合。

Method: 摘要说明本项资助支持分析X射线多镜任务（XMM）的数据，并已获得观测时间用于观测两颗年轻中子星B1823-13和B1046-58；其中对B1823-13的观测已经进行并完成分析。摘要未说明具体的观测频段、时间跨度、样本量或数据分析方法细节。

Result: 对B1823-13的观测已经开展并完成分析。中心核表现出未脉冲化的延展非热性质；弥散成分的温度过高，无法解释为热辐射。摘要未说明具体数值、显著性或其他定量结果。

Conclusion: 作者认为，中心核的未脉冲化延展非热性质表明该延展辐射源对应于脉冲星驱动的星云所发出的同步辐射；而温度过高的弥散延展成分则被解释为来自周围超新星遗迹壳层的非热辐射。至于这些结果与脉冲星计时、自转减慢力矩变化、单脉冲、偏振、射电谱、磁星或快速射电暴（FRB）的联系，根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: This grant is to support analysis of data from the X-ray Multi-mirror Mission (XMM). Specifically, we have been awarded time to observe two young neutron stars, B1823-13 and B1046-58, whose X-ray emission is expected to be a complicated combination of emission from an associated supernova remnant, from a wind-powered synchrotron nebula, from magnetospheric pulsations, and from the surface of the neutron star itself. It is only with XMM's unique combination of spectral, temporal and angular resolution that all these different processes can be separated and studied. Observations of B1823-13 have been conducted and analyzed. Abstract: Gamma-ray bursts (GRBs) are extremely energetic explosions at cosmological distances. We have made great progress in understanding the mysteries of these events since they were discovered more than forty years ago. However, some open questions still remain, e.g. how many classes of GRBs are there, what are the progenitors of these classes, and what is the central engine powering these huge explosions? Thanks to the NASA missions Swift and Fermi, which are used to detect the multi-wavelength emission from these transients, our understanding of GRBs has been greatly advanced. In this dissertation, I use multi-wavelength data to constrain the progenitor and central engine of GRBs. My dissertation consists of three parts: (1) By adding the third dimension "amplitude" as a complementary criterion in classifying GRBs, we test whether some short GRBs are "tip-of-iceberg" of long GRBs, and explain why some high redshift long GRBs have short durations in the rest frame. (2) Using Swift data, we investigate whether the data are consistent with the hypothesis that there exist millisecond magnetar central engines in some long GRBs. We reach the conclusion that at least some long GRBs have a magnetar central engine. (3) We test how well the data are consistent with the magnetar central engine model for short GRBs. We identify that a good fraction of short GRBs have a supra-massive magnetar central engine, which collapses to a black hole after hundreds of seconds. We use the data to constrain the neutron star equation of state.

</details>
